# Skills · S · part 37

765 entries, sci-flat to Science. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sci-flat**: Makes calm, precise 2D science-explainer videos in a flat vector "modern textbook diagram" style (navy ink on off-white paper, three accents, uniform rounded strokes, leader-line labels, numbered callouts, elements that draw on and pop in). Use it for any "how X works", "the science of X", process, cycle, reaction, anatomy or cause-and-effect explainer that should look like an editorial diagram rather than a cartoon or a 3D render. ([owlinthebarnai/video-studio-kit](https://github.com/owlinthebarnai/video-studio-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-flat) · ★142)
- **sci-floating-point-and-numerical-foundations**: Use when a numerical result is wrong, surprising, or not reproducible: IEEE 754 floating point and what you are actually computing with, catastrophic cancellation, NaN and signed zero, comparison and summation practices, conditioning and numerical stability and the difference between them, and the BLAS/LAPACK substrate that everything else stands on. Includes the router for the whole math-science-programming reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/math-science-programming/skills/sci-floating-point-and-numerical-foundations) · ★5)
- **sci-fluoro**: Makes science-explainer videos that look like confocal / fluorescence microscopy footage - glowing channel-coloured structures (cyan, magenta, yellow, green) on pure black, additive light, bloom, sensor grain, monospace instrument read-outs, and focus pulls between beats. Use it for any "under the microscope" explainer (biology, chemistry, materials, everyday phenomena seen at cell or molecule scale) rendered as a Canvas 2D frame page with video-studio/tools/page-render.mjs. ([owlinthebarnai/video-studio-kit](https://github.com/owlinthebarnai/video-studio-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-fluoro) · ★142)
- **sci-form**: Use for implementing, debugging, reviewing, or extending sci-form across Rust, Python, WASM/TypeScript, and CLI. Covers conformer generation, quantum chemistry, spectroscopy, materials, ML, and the test/release workflow. ([jigonzalez930209/sci-form](https://github.com/jigonzalez930209/sci-form/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-form) · ★4)
- **sci-framing**: Use when locking the conceptual advance and the "why now" before drafting for Science (AAAS) — converts a correct result into a Science-shaped narrative that leads with the broad advance rather than the background, and seeds the title, abstract, and cover letter. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Science-Skills/skills/sci-framing) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

- **sci-index**: 수집한 논문 폴더(papers/{paper_id}/)에서 서지정보 색인 index.csv를 만들고 검수한다. 편수와 관계없이 sci-retr 수집 뒤 항상 실행한다(LLM 없음). "색인 만들어", "논문 목록 정리해", "이 폴더에 무슨 논문 있어", "관련 논문 찾아줘"에 사용하며 리버·김리버·김리버씨·댕댕아·댕댕이·김댕댕·김댕댕씨 호칭이 앞에 붙어도 적용한다. 한국어 한 줄 요약은 사용자가 원할 때만 sci-tldr가 만든다. ([angmond1/sci](https://github.com/angmond1/sci/tree/HEAD/sci-index))
- **sci-innovation-selection**: 在 SCI 项目中按需筛选研究选题、核查创新点、比较候选机制或规划最小验证；适用于 LLM、VLM、CV、深度学习、边缘计算和电子信息交叉研究。普通论文解读、翻译、润色及代码修改不触发全流程。 ([AIM135D/sci-research-workflow](https://github.com/AIM135D/sci-research-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-innovation-selection))
- **sci-introduction**: 写"国内外研究现状"（文献综述 / Related Work）。当用户提到研究现状、文献综述、国内外研究对比、Literature Review、述评、研究背景、国内外研究现状时使用。即使用户没有明确说"写研究现状"，只要涉及对某一主题的国内外学术研究现状进行结构化梳理，就应该触发此技能。 ([stephenlzc/sci-introduction](https://github.com/stephenlzc/sci-introduction/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★9)
- **sci-issue-intake**: Use when a SCI paper task begins from a vague request, reviewer comment, advisor feedback, suspected flaw, manuscript confusion, or multi-step repair need ([keithhegit/sci-using-kit](https://github.com/keithhegit/sci-using-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-issue-intake) · ★1)

- **sci-journal-search**: 查询 SCI 期刊的详细信息，包括新锐分区（中科院分区）、JCR 分区等。 ([songxf1024/skills](https://github.com/songxf1024/skills/tree/HEAD/sci-journal-search))
- **sci-journal-submission-expert**: SCI 期刊投稿全流程专家支持：预审诊断、梯度选刊、作者指南格式核查、数据与开放科学合规、审稿意见解析与逐点回复、Cover Letter 与投稿策略、出版伦理与合规边界、跨境 APC/豁免与发票流程、投稿系统状态与催稿策略、多刊并行规划。在用户提出选刊、desk rejection 风险、统计/报告规范、回复信、APC、预印本/署名/利益冲突等问题时触发。不用于：数据造假、图片篡改、一稿多投、虚假同行评审、代签版权/法律文件、或代替用户做最终学术结论。 ([PancrePal-xiaoyibao/VitaForge](https://github.com/PancrePal-xiaoyibao/VitaForge/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/sci-journal-submission-expert) · ★6 · 3 copies)

Router matches the user's problem description against recipe keywords and returns the best fit. - **sci-paper-writing**: 地理学、遥感、土壤、生态方向 SCI 英文研究论文的写作与修改。覆盖三类研究论文：时空变化（趋势、变化检测、驱动归因）、定量反演与模拟（土壤盐分、SOC、土壤水分、植被参数等）、观测对照（处理与对照、野外实验）。对标新锐分区地理学相关 1 区期刊（RSE、ISPRS J、TGRS、JAG、IJGIS、Catena、Geoderma、Ecological Indicators 等）。用于选题与创新点判断、选刊、标题、摘要、Highlights、引言、研究区、数据与方法、精度验证、结果、讨论、结论、图件、去 AI 味润色、cover letter 与投稿自查。用户提到写/改/润色/审查地理或遥感论文的任何部分、问"创新点够不够""投哪本刊""引言怎么写""讨论怎么写""SHAP 怎么写""怎么去 AI 味"时使用。综述与 Meta 分析用 geo-review-writing；回复审稿意见用 geo-reviewer-response；建立个人写作风格档案用 geo-writing-style。 ([Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors](https://github.com/Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-paper-writing) · ★62)

- **sci-peer-review**: 只读审查科研论文或研究方案，依据实际材料分析主张、方法、实验、统计、引用和可复现性，输出有位置和修正建议的意见；不代替期刊审稿或修改原稿。 ([AIM135D/sci-research-workflow](https://github.com/AIM135D/sci-research-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-peer-review))
- **sci-planner**: Create a verified, actionable plan for a task by spawning a planner agent and a plan-checker agent with research context. - **sci-playground-builder**: 为「科学小游戏大冒险」(sci-playground) 设计、实现与验收新的 STEAM 互动科学实验、新学科展馆、「小念问爸爸」语音问答与「科学百宝箱」大师故事卡。凡在本仓库扩充游戏、分类、百科或优化互动实验体验时调用。 ([handsondad/sci-playground](https://github.com/handsondad/sci-playground/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-playground-builder) · 2 copies)
- **sci-plot**: Scientific figure prompt engine. Analyzes papers/projects/algorithms/code/documents, matches against a dual-track rules pool (local & cloud/external skills), applies the Scope Isolation ("烟消云散") rule, and generates high-quality, cross-platform image generation prompts. Users copy prompts into ChatGPT/DALL-E, Doubao, Qwen, Midjourney, Stable Diffusion, or use them as guides for SVG/Matlab/Origin. Does not generate images directly — produces prompts only for maximum flexibility and environmental independence. ([LPK3215/sci-plot](https://github.com/LPK3215/sci-plot/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
- **sci-polish**: Two-stage academic paper polishing skill. Stage A reduces AI detection traces (targeting GPTZero, Turnitin, Originality.ai). Use whenever the user asks to polish, proofread, humanize, reduce AI rate, improve writing quality, or prepare a paper for submission. - **sci-research-refine**: 在 SCI 创新筛选中细化候选研究机制的单 Agent 阶段，保持问题锚点和证据边界。 ([AIM135D/sci-research-workflow](https://github.com/AIM135D/sci-research-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-research-refine))
- **sci-retr**: Sci Retriever(sci-retr) — DOI 목록이나 첨부 엑셀·WoS·Scopus 검색 결과의 논문 본문 PDF, 본문 텍스트, SI 를 수집한다. 논문 PDF·링크의 reference(참고문헌) 논문도 수집한다. "논문 받아줘", "엑셀 논문 리스트 수집해줘", "reference들 모두 수집해줘", "DOI 수집", "원문 다운로드", "SI 저장", "sci-retr 사용법"에 사용. 앞에 리버·김리버·김리버씨·댕댕아·댕댕이·김댕댕·김댕댕씨 호칭이 붙어도 수집 요청으로 인식한다. 수집 뒤 sci-index 색인은 항상 만들고, 한국어 한 줄 요약은 원할 때만 sci-tldr를 쓴다. ([angmond1/sci](https://github.com/angmond1/sci/tree/HEAD/sci-retr))

- **sci-review**: Specialized workflows for drafting, refining, and responding to academic literature reviews and peer review feedback. Use this skill for literature review outlines, research-gap synthesis, reviewer rebuttals, response letters, and academic writing tone repair. ([ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write](https://github.com/ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review) · ★225)
- **sci-review-benchmark**: 找 3–8 篇 标杆综述（同主题/同方法/顶刊），解构其结构、检索策略、章节、图表与写作风格，提炼借鉴清单。可选用 paper-lookup (K-Dense-AI) 做被引/施引网络分析，定位高质量标杆。Use when the user wants to learn from top reviews in a field, benchmark a review against top journals, study high-impact review article structure, or "find highly-cited reviews on X". ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-benchmark) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch-rest**: 写综述 Results / Discussion / Conclusion 三章 + references.bib，每节首尾有过渡句，图编号一致，discussion 不重复 results。Use when the user wants to write the results, discussion, and conclusion of a review, or export references in journal style. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch-rest) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch1**: 写综述 Introduction 章：漏斗式结构（broad → narrow → gap → aim），全部引用真实文献。Use when the user wants to write the introduction of a review article, draft the background section, or state the aim of a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch1) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch2**: 写综述 Methods 章：检索策略、筛选、数据提取、合成方法、PRISMA 数字闭环、PRISMA 2020/GRADE/SWiM 引用。Use when the user wants to write the methods section of a review, document the search strategy, or build a PRISMA-aligned methods chapter. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch2) · ★1)
- **sci-review-cover-letter**: 写 Cover Letter（投稿信）+ Highlights + Graphical Abstract 文字版 + 推荐/排除审稿人，符合期刊字数与格式。Use when the user wants to write a cover letter to editor, draft highlights, or propose reviewers. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-cover-letter) · ★1)
- **sci-review-expert**: SCI 综述全流程总控。负责按 立项 → 检索 → 选题 → 对标 → 筛选 → Zotero → 精读 → 框架 → 配图 → 章节 → 选刊 → 规范 → 润色 → 授权 → 投稿信 → 自检 → 审稿回复 顺序编排 21 个子 skill，维护项目状态机、用户交互、每阶段门禁判定、异常回退。Use when the user wants to write a systematic review or narrative review for an SCI journal, says 帮我写综述 / 启动综述流程 / 综述全流程 / run review pipeline / SCI review workflow, or asks Codex to coordinate all review sub-skills. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-expert) · ★1)
- **sci-review-figure-license**: 对非自创且非开放许可的图发起授权申请，写邮件模板、跟踪回复、维护 license_status.csv。Use when the user wants to request figure permission, write a copyright permission email, or track image licenses for a manuscript. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-figure-license) · ★1)
- **sci-review-figures**: 制作综述所需图（PRISMA、概念图、时间线、证据汇总表、机制图），输出矢量+位图双版本并管理图注与授权状态。Use when the user wants to make PRISMA flow diagram, draw a conceptual figure, build an evidence summary table, or design figures for a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-figures) · ★1)
- **sci-review-framework**: 把素材组织成可写的章节大纲、故事线与配图计划，做逻辑自洽与重复检查。Use when the user wants to outline a review, build a review framework, design chapter structure, or plan figures for a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-framework) · ★1)
- **sci-review-fulltext**: 对每篇纳入文献做结构化精读，提取 8 要素笔记与横向证据表，标注可入综述的论点。Use when the user wants to extract data from PDFs, build an evidence table for a review, do structured reading notes, or align papers to a review framework. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-fulltext) · ★1)
- **sci-review-init**: 综述项目初始化。收集研究方向、用途、截止时间、目标期刊范围，建立工作目录结构与 project_meta.yaml / timeline.md / risk_register.md / project_state.json。Use when the user wants to start a SCI review, kick off a systematic review, or set up project meta and timeline. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-init) · ★1)
- **sci-review-journal-pick**: 用 LetPub / Elsevier Finder / Springer Suggester 找候选期刊，按 scope/IF/速度/费用/风险五维评分，推荐 3 主推 + 2 备选。Use when the user wants to pick a target journal for a review, compare IF and review speed, or shortlist journals in a field. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-journal-pick) · ★1)
- **sci-review-journal-rules**: 把锁定期刊的 Author Guidelines 逐条落到稿件上，输出 ≥30 项可勾选规则清单与差异处置。Use when the user wants to check journal submission guidelines, align a manuscript to a target journal, or build a compliance gap list. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-journal-rules) · ★1)
- **sci-review-polish**: 按目标期刊语言与风格精修稿件，统一术语与缩写、句法优化、查重控制，输出润色稿与 polish_log。Use when the user wants to polish a manuscript, run an iThenticate check, fix English writing, or unify terminology. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-polish) · ★1)
- **sci-review-rebuttal**: 应对大修/小修/拒稿转投的审稿意见，逐点回复、生成修订稿与改动定位表，保留 track changes。Use when the user wants to respond to reviewer comments, write a rebuttal letter, prepare a revised manuscript, or handle a major/minor revision. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-rebuttal) · ★1)
- **sci-review-screening**: 按 PRISMA 2020 流程做文献初筛（标题/摘要）与复筛（全文），双人筛选并算 kappa，输出 PRISMA 流程图与最终纳入清单。Use when the user wants to screen references by inclusion/exclusion criteria, build a PRISMA flow diagram, or do dual-reviewer screening. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-screening) · ★1)
- **sci-review-search-builder**: 把研究问题转成 PubMed / Web of Science / Scopus / Embase / Cochrane 等数据库可复用的检索式，含概念块拆分、同义词/MeSH 补全、布尔组合与每库语法适配。Use when the user wants to build a literature search string, design a systematic search strategy, generate PubMed or WoS queries, or needs MeSH/Emtree terms. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-search-builder) · ★1)
- **sci-review-search-runner**: 执行多库检索、合并去重、导出 RIS/CSV/BibTeX 元数据与摘要、记录 PRISMA 计数。优先调用可选的 paper-lookup (K-Dense-AI) 做跨库统一检索,否则 fallback 到 E-Utils + Crossref + Semantic Scholar。Use when the user wants to run a literature search, deduplicate references, export from PubMed/WoS/Scopus, build a PRISMA identification count, or "look up papers on X", "find DOI", "who cites this paper". ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-search-runner) · ★1)

([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-zotero-pdf) · ★1)

- **sci-search**: Поиск и верификация внешних фактов по первоисточникам: DOI, стандарты (ГОСТ/ISO/IEC), нормативы (НРБ/ICRP/IAEA), патенты, datasheet, ядерные данные. Обязателен ЛЮБОМУ агенту перед тем, как назвать число, формулу, норматив или ссылку — память модели источником НЕ является. Триггеры: «чему равен», «какой норматив/предел», «найди источник», «проверь ссылку», «действует ли ГОСТ». Один факт/число/ссылка — сюда; большой обзор → deep-research; своя библиотека дозиметрии → library; личные документы оператора → приватный RAG-скилл. - **sci2doc**: 用于将已成稿的 SCI 论文材料转化为中文博士或硕士学位论文草稿。技能采用原子化 Markdown 工作流，逐节撰写后合并导出 Word，执行严格的章节结构校验、逐阶段门禁检查与版本快照回滚。正文字数按学位类型设定软目标（博士不低于五万字，硕士不低于三万字），材料不足时宁可少写，不以编造数据充抵字数。格式支持内置模板与自定义院校模板两种模式，自定义模板信息不完整时仅允许整理 Markdown，不予导出 docx。触发词：博士论文、硕士论文、学位论文、毕业论文、SCI转论文、doctoral thesis、master thesis、dissertation。本技能要求用户提供可访问的 SCI 论文材料，不适用于本科毕业论文。 ([wsxwj123/opencode-skills-backup](https://github.com/wsxwj123/opencode-skills-backup/tree/HEAD/sci2doc) · ★7)

For regulatory variant scoring use regulomedb-database; for GWAS associations use gwas-database. - **sciagent-gget-genomic-databases**: Unified CLI/Python interface to 20+ genomic databases. Gene lookups (Ensembl search/info/seq), BLAST/BLAT, AlphaFold, Enrichr enrichment, OpenTargets disease/drug, CELLxGENE single-cell, cBioPortal/COSMIC cancer, ARCHS4 expression. Spans genomics, proteomics, disease. For batch/advanced BLAST use biopython; for multi-DB Python SDK use bioservices. ([ai4nucleome/BioMaster](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/BioMaster/tree/HEAD/.opencode/bioskills/sciagent-gget-genomic-databases) · ★125)
- **sciagent-harmony-batch-correction**: Harmony batch correction for scRNA-seq and other omics. Removes batch effects from PCA embeddings while preserving biology. Run after PCA, before UMAP. ([ai4nucleome/BioMaster](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/BioMaster/tree/HEAD/.opencode/bioskills/sciagent-harmony-batch-correction) · ★125)

- **sciagent-mofaplus-multi-omics**: Multi-Omics Factor Analysis v2 (MOFA+) with mofapy2. Jointly decompose omics layers (scRNA, ATAC, proteomics, methylation) into latent factors capturing major variation. Multi-group designs. AnnData views → MOFA object → train → variance explained → correlate factors with metadata → visualize/cluster → enrich top loadings. ([ai4nucleome/BioMaster](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/BioMaster/tree/HEAD/.opencode/bioskills/sciagent-mofaplus-multi-omics) · ★125)
- **sciagent-muon-multiomics-singlecell**: Multi-modal single-cell analysis with muon/MuData. Joint RNA+ATAC (10x Multiome), CITE-seq (RNA+protein), other multi-omics. - **sciagent-popv-cell-annotation**: Consensus cell type annotation: runs 10+ algorithms (KNN-Harmony/BBKNN/Scanorama/scVI, CellTypist, ONCLASS, Random Forest, SCANVI, SVM, XGBoost) on a labeled reference and transfers labels via majority voting. Outputs per-method labels, consensus, agreement score. Use when single-method annotation is insufficient or you need ensemble uncertainty for novel states. - **sciagent-remap-database**: Query ReMap 2022 TF ChIP-seq peak database via REST API and BED downloads. Use for TF co-occupancy, regulatory annotation, and TF binding atlases. Use jaspar-database for PWM motifs; encode-database for ENCODE tracks. - **sciai-batch-review**: Review a set of split sub-plans: runs sciai-plan-review on each sub-plan in parallel, then performs an integration review to check cross-plan handoffs, gaps, and overall coherence. TRIGGER when: user explicitly invokes /sciai-batch-review, says 'review all sub-plans', 'review the split plans', 'batch review', or wants to review the output of sciai-plan-split as a whole. DO NOT trigger for single-plan review — that's sciai-plan-review. ([JakeWeinstein/SciAi](https://github.com/JakeWeinstein/SciAi/tree/HEAD/sciai-batch-review) · ★1)
- **sciai-plan-review**: Multi-level plan review using independent parallel agents. Asks the user to pick a thoroughness level (1=Sonnet, 2=Sonnet+Opus, 3=Sonnet+Opus+Codex), then launches independent reviewers in parallel to evaluate a plan for completeness, feasibility, and alignment with the user's goals. TRIGGER when: user explicitly invokes /sciai-plan-review, says 'review this plan', 'check this plan', 'is this plan good', or when called by the sci-planner skill after plan creation. DO NOT trigger on requests to create plans — that's sci-planner's job. ([JakeWeinstein/SciAi](https://github.com/JakeWeinstein/SciAi/tree/HEAD/sciai-plan-review) · ★1)
- **sciai-plan-split**: Split a large plan into parallel and serial sub-plans for independent execution. Identifies parallelism opportunities, subdivides further if chunks are still too large, then verifies full coverage with a Sonnet agent. TRIGGER when: user explicitly invokes /sciai-plan-split, says 'split this plan', 'break this plan up', or when called by sciai-plan-review after flagging a plan as too large. DO NOT trigger on requests to create or review plans — those are sci-planner and sciai-plan-review. ([JakeWeinstein/SciAi](https://github.com/JakeWeinstein/SciAi/tree/HEAD/sciai-plan-split) · ★1)
- **sciai-verify**: Multi-level correctness verification for code. Asks the user to pick a thoroughness level (1=Sonnet, 2=Sonnet+Opus, 3=Sonnet+Opus+Codex), then launches independent agents in parallel to audit code and reconciles findings into a brief report. Automatically splits large scopes into chunks so each agent gets a focused review. TRIGGER when: user explicitly invokes /sciai-verify, says 'verify the correctness', 'verify this code', 'audit this for correctness', 'double-check this implementation', or asks for independent verification of code that was just created or edited.
